Derivative wrt h_i:
From source: h_i appears as a[m+k] when i = m+k => m=i-k. Term (1-h[m]). Valid m in [0,n-1]. Thus contribution S_i = ∑_{k: 0<=i-k<=n-1? also valid m} w_k (1-h[i-k]) = ∑_{m:0<=m<=n-1} w_{i-m} (1-h_m) where k=i-m within valid range [-n+1,n-1].
From target: h_i appears as h[m] when m=i. Term -h[m+k] = - h[i+k]. Valid if 0<=i+k<=n-1. Contribution T_i = -∑_{k:0<=i+k<=n-1} w_k h[i+k].
So grad_i = ∑_m w_{i-m} - ∑_m w_{i-m}h_m - ∑_k w_k h[i+k]. First term = (w * ones)_i? with indices. The negative terms are correlation/convolution of w with h. Could compute via FFT for dense w. We can derive delta for active lags more directly:
For actual c_k = ∑ h[m+k](1-h[m]) (source at m+k). Swap i 1->0, j 0->1.
Delta for a given k:
- Source i removed: if valid m=i-k (0<=m<n), -1 if h[m] old=0? Since source term h_i(1-h_m), h_i old1, after0: removal = -(1-h_{i-k}) if valid.
- Source j added: + (1-h_{j-k}) if valid.
- Target i: (1-h_i) changes 0->1. It affects source m=i-k? Wait target m=i: term h_{i+k}(1-h_i), change +h_{i+k} if valid (0<=i+k<n).
- Target j: change -h_{j+k} if valid.
So delta_k = [valid j-k](1 - h[j-k]) - [valid i-k](1 - h[i-k]) + [valid i+k] h[i+k] - [valid j+k] h[j+k]. All h old. This is O(1) per k! Great. Our slice formula double counts self/source-target same. We can avoid by first remove source i slice, add source j, add target i, subtract target j, then correct overlaps:

But also source_i target_j actual -1 at lag i-j; our contributions -1; no fix. Let's test with n=2 example i=1,j=0: source+target raw gave delta [0,2,-2]? Let's formulate delta as direct: For a swap i (1->0), j (0->1). For each lag l = n-1+k, the affected old/new values for combinations of source s and target m where s or m in {i,j}. Since typical affected only one unless lags near. We can compute delta for active lags O(1) with cases using old h. For full update, we can compute by recomputing full c via FFT after accepted swap rather than O(n) delta. That may be simpler! Full c via FFT is straightforward. For local search, to test candidate (i,j), we can compute delta on active lags O(A) using direct formula for each k; then if promising, evaluate full new c via FFT or exact correlate? That could be many FFTs. We can instead after accepting update counts by O(n) direct correct formula. Let's derive correct full delta cleanly.

Use c_k = ∑_{m} h[m+k] (1-h[m]) with valid.
Define indicator terms. Old h. New h' after swap:
h'_i=0, h'_j=1, others same.
Delta_k = ∑ [h'_s(1-h'_m) - h_s(1-h_m)] over pairs (m,s) with s=m+k.
Linearity: For each changed position, contributions to terms where it is source and/or target. Use inclusion-exclusion:
Let Δ_source(p) = h'_p - h_p = -1 for p=i, +1 for p=j. Contribution to c_k from source p with target m=p-k: Δ_source(p)*(1-h_m) but if target also changed, using old 1-h_m not h'_m, need correction.
Let Δ_target(m) = (1-h'_m)-(1-h_m) = +1 for m=i (since 1-0 - (1-1)=1), -1 for m=j.
Then delta = ∑_p Δ_source(p)(1-h_{p-k}) + ∑_m Δ_target(m) h_{m+k} + corrections for pairs where both p and m changed: For term (p,m), formula uses Δ_source(p)*(1-h_m) + Δ_target(m)*h_p, but true delta = (h_p+Δp)(1-h_m+Δt) - h_p(1-h_m) = Δp(1-h_m)+Δt h_p + Δp Δt. So correction Δp Δt. Thus direct:
```
delta_k = 
 -1*(1-h[i-k]) if valid + 1*(1-h[j-k]) if valid
 +1*h[i+k] if valid + (-1)*h[j+k] if valid
 + Δ_i Δ_t for term where source i target i (k=0): (-1)*(+1) = -1 (if i valid source/target same? k=0)
 + for source i target j: if k = i - j (s=i,m=j): Δp=-1, Δt=-1 (target j), product +1. Wait target j Δt=-1, product (-1)*(-1)=+1. True delta -1. Formula without correction: source i: -(1-h_j)= -1; target j: -h_i=-1; total -2; correction +1 => -1. Good.
 + for source j target i: if k = j - i: Δp=+1, Δt=+1 (target i), product +1. Without correction source j +(1-h_i)=0; target i +h_j=0; correction +1 => +1.
 + for source j target j: k=0: Δp=+1, Δt=-1 product -1. Without correction source j +(1-h_j)=1; target j -h_j=0; correction -1 =>0.
 + for source i target i: k=0: product -1. Without correction source i -(1-h_i)=0; target i +h_i=1; correction -1 =>0.
```
So full delta formula with corrections:
```
delta = zeros
# source i removal: indices for k=i-m, l=n-1+i-m, m=0..n-1 => slice i: n+i ascending values (1-h)[::-1], add -
delta[i:n+i] -= (1-h)[::-1]
# source j add: delta[j:n+j] += (1-h)[::-1]
# target i add (+1): slice n-1-i : 2n-1-i add h
delta[n-1-i:2n-1-i] += h
# target j remove (-1): delta[n-1-j:2n-1-j] -= h
# corrections:
delta[n-1] -= 1  # source i target i
delta[n-1] -= 1  # source j target j (if i != j yes both self) => -2
if i != j:
    k_ij = i - j; l = n-1 + k_ij
    if 0 <= l < len: delta[l] += 1  # source i target j
    k_ji = j - i; l = n-1 + k_ji
    if 0 <= l < len: delta[l] += 1  # source j target i
```
Wait correction for source j target i also +1? In example i=1,j=0, corrections: index1 -2; l for i-j=1 index2 +=1; l for j-i=-1 index0 +=1. Raw [0,2,-2] -> [1,0,-1] expected. Good. For random? Great. But note source i target j and source j target i lags may coincide with k=0 if i=j (not). They are distinct one positive one negative. Corrections each +1. We can implement full delta O(n) vectorized as above. This is correct for binary swap.

For fractional not binary, swap not. But we can quantize to binary.

Now local search algorithm using this delta. We need choose i (one) and j (zero). We can compute delta on active lags only first. For each active lag index l (actual k), we can compute delta via O(1) cases:
```
def delta_k_single(l, h, i, j):
 k = l - (n-1)
 val=0
 # source i if 0<=i-k<n -> m=i-k valid
 if 0 <= i-k < n: val -= (1-h[i-k])
 if 0 <= j-k < n: val += (1-h[j-k])
 if 0 <= i+k < n: val += h[i+k]
 if 0 <= j+k < n: val -= h[j+k]
 if k==0: val -=2
 if k==i-j: val +=1
 if k==j-i: val +=1
 return val
```
For binary h_i=1,h_j=0, but formula uses h old (works).

def make_binary(h,n):
    order=np.argsort(-h)
    b=np.zeros(n); b[order[:n//2]]=1.0; # ensures sum exact if n even
    # handle ties? okay
    return b
```

    # If one full-improvement pass finds no improvement, reduce slack? or random anneal.

Simpler candidate evaluation per (i,j): compute `ks = active - (n-1)` vector length A. Compute delta vectorized for all active:
```
def candidate_delta(i,j,ks,h,n):
  val = np.zeros(A)
  # source i: m=i-k valid -> add -(1-h[m])
  mask = (0 <= i-ks) & (i-ks < n)
  val[mask] -= 1.0 - h[i-ks[mask]]
  # source j +
  mask = (0 <= j-ks) & ...
  val[mask] += 1.0 - h[j-ks[mask]]
  # target i +
  mask = (0 <= i+ks) & ...
  val[mask] += h[i+ks[mask]]
  # target j -
  mask = (0 <= j+ks) & ...
  val[mask] -= h[j+ks[mask]]
  # corrections:
  val[ks==0] -= 2
  mask = ks == (i-j); val[mask] +=1
  mask = ks == (j-i); val[mask] +=1
  return val
```

After candidate chosen, update counts full with correct delta O(n). Need `full_swap_delta(h,i,j,n)` vectorized:
```
def swap_delta_counts(h,i,j,n):
  delta=np.zeros(2*n-1)
  rev = (1-h)[::-1] # length n, compute each time? O(n).
  delta[i:n+i] -= rev
  delta[j:n+j] += rev
  delta[n-1-i : 2*n-1-i] += h
  delta[n-1-j : 2*n-1-j] -= h
  L=len(delta)
  delta[n-1] -= 2.0
  l1 = n-1 + (i-j)
  if 0 <= l1 < L: delta[l1] += 1.0
  l2 = n-1 + (j-i)
  if 0 <= l2 < L: delta[l2] += 1.0
  return delta
```
Let's verify slice lengths: `delta[i:n+i]` length n? If i=n-1, n+i=2n-1 exclusive, last index 2n-2 length n. If i=0, 0:n. Good. Target slice `n-1-i : 2*n-1-i`: if i=n-1, start0, end n, length n. if i=0, start n-1,end2n-1 length n. Good.
After applying `counts += delta`, h[i],h[j] swapped. Need ensure counts within numeric integer; due floating errors, maybe round? We can keep float and maybe `counts = np.where((counts>0)&(counts<1e-9),0, counts)`. But local search depends max. Could periodically recompute full counts via FFT for consistency.

Algorithm iteration:
1. Compute gradient from active M (all max).
2. Build candidate pool (pos top p, neg top n, plus random maybe 20).
3. For each candidate in pool, compute estimated new max using active only (counts + delta). Track candidate with min est_max, and if est_max < M-0.5 (integer reduction) or < M maybe.
4. For top `test_limit` candidates sorted by est_max (or all if small), apply swapped h and full delta O(n) to counts (or recompute FFT for current candidate counts) to get exact new max. If exact new max < M - 1e-6 (or tie with lexicographic), accept update.
5. If no improvement after some tries, try random candidate pool or simulated annealing accepting small worsening to escape, with cooling. But we need final best, can run random anneal with memory.

def binary_local_search(h_bin, n, deadline, max_iters=??, alpha? not):
  h = make_binary(h_bin,n)
  counts = full_c(h,n)
  # numeric clean
  counts = np.round(counts) if close? Maybe use as float.
  M = counts.max()
  best_counts=counts.copy(); best_bit=h.copy(); bestM=M
  ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.where(h<=0.5)[0]
  # we need maintain arrays lists; use Python sets for random removals? For gradient top, arrays.
  no_improve=0
  while time < deadline:
    # active
    tol=1e-6
    active = np.where(counts >= M - 1e-6)[0]
    if len(active)>40: active = active np.argsort(-counts[active])[:40]
    if len(active)==0: break
    # gradient subgradient
    weights = np.ones(len(active))/len(active)
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    for idx in active: formula add weights
    pos = grad[ones] > 0 -> indices among h=1
    neg = grad[zeros] < 0 -> zeros
    # select top pos by grad, top neg by -grad
    pool = list product top 12/12 plus random 20
    # If no pos/neg, random pairs
    # Evaluate candidates:
       best_est = inf; candidates_list=[]
       for i,j in pool:
          # quick if i==j skip
          delta_act = candidate_delta_act(...)
          est = (counts[active] + delta_act).max()

       sort candidates by est

       for cand: apply swap to temporary? We can update counts in place and revert if fail.
          apply_delta = swap_delta_counts(h,i,j,n)
          new_counts = counts + apply_delta
          newM = new_counts.max()
          if newM < M - 1e-6 or (abs(newM-M)< and sorted top decreases): accept
             h[i],h[j]=0,1; counts=new_counts; update M; ones,zeros update; no_improve=0
             if M < bestM: best... break
          else revert counts
    if no accepted:
       no_improve++
       # try simulated anneal: random swap that doesn't increase M by >1 maybe, with probability, or perturb active tolerance to M-1 to reduce secondary
       if no_improve > 50: random restart? or switch to active slack 1.

```
def dense_smooth_grad_from_c(c, w, n):
   # c length L=2n-1, w same sum1
   # A_i = sum_{r=0}^{n-1} w[i+r]
   # B_i = sliding_corr_shift(w, h)?? B_i = ∑_m w[i+n-1-m] h_m = conv(w, h_rev)[i+n-1]? We need arr? B_i formula with h_m directly. Y_i = ∑_{r=0}^{n-1} w[i+r] arr[r]. For B, we have w[i+n-1-m] h_m. Let r=n-1-m -> w[i+r] h_{n-1-r} = ∑_r w[i+r] h_rev[r]. So B = sliding_corr(w, h[::-1]).
   # C_i = ∑_s w[n-1+s-i] h_s = ∑_r w_rev[i+r] h_rev[r] (as above). So C = sliding_corr(w[::-1], h[::-1]).
   # A = sliding_corr(w, ones) but ones_rev=ones; since constant, can compute with conv or sliding sum via prefix.
```
`sliding_corr(w, arr)` where arr length n, returns Y_i for i=0..n-1 = ∑_{r=0}^{n-1} w[i+r] arr[r]. Use convolution of w and arr_rev:
```
def sliding_corr(w, arr, n):
    arr_rev = arr[::-1]
    size = next_power2(len(w)+len(arr)-1)
    Fw=np.rfft(w,size); Fa=np.rfft(arr_rev,size)
    out=np.irfft(Fw*Fa,size)
    return out[n-1:2*n-1] ? index i+n-1.
```
Length w L=2n-1, arr n, conv len 3n-2. Index i+n-1 for i=0..n-1 gives n elements from n-1 to 2n-2. Yes.
A can use prefix sums: `A = np.cumsum(w reversed?)` Let's derive A_i = ∑_{r=0}^{n-1} w[i+r]. Use cumulative: total prefix P[0]=0; A_i = P[i+n]-P[i]. Since w length L=2n-1, i+n <=2n-1 for i=n? For i=n-1, i+n=2n-1 exclusive ok. So prefix easy.
B: arr = h[::-1]. C: arr = h[::-1], w = w[::-1].
Then grad = A - B - C.
Let's test n=2 with w. h=[a,b], h_rev=[b,a]. sliding_corr(w, h_rev):
Y0=∑ r w[0+r] h_rev[r] = w0*b + w1*a. But B_a earlier w1*a+w0*b yes.
Y1=w1*a + w2*b. B_b =? For i=1, sum w[1+r]h_rev[r]=w1*b? Wait r0 w1*b? h_rev0=b, r1 w2*a. B_b expected ∑_m w[1+1-m]h_m = m0 w2 b? m1 w1 a? w2*b + w1*a. Yes. Good.

def run_dense_lbfgs(h, n, deadline, max_total_s=None, alphas=None, mus=None):
   best_h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5_fast_or_direct
   bounds=...
   for mu in mus:
      for alpha in alphas:
         remaining=deadline-time.time()
         if remaining<30: break
         maxit=min(800, int(remaining*0.8?))
         try:
            res=minimize(dense_obj, best_h, jac=dense_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, ftol=1e-18, gtol=1e-12, maxiter=maxit)
            hnew=project_feasible(res.x,n)
            cnew=compute_c5_fast(hnew,n) # maybe use direct if n<=1024 else FFT
            if cnew < best_c + 1e-12:
                best_h=hnew; best_c=cnew
         except: pass
   return best_h,best_c
```

Implement `sliding_corr(w, arr)`:
```
def sliding_corr(w, arr):
    n = len(arr)
    size=1; L=len(w)+n-1
    while size<L: size<<=1
    return np.irfft(np.rfft(w,size)*np.rfft(arr[::-1],size), size)[n-1:2*n-1]
```
`out[i+n-1]` for i=0..n-1 corresponds slice [n-1:2*n-1]? Length n: start n-1, stop 2n-1 exclusive gives indices n-1..2n-2. Yes. Good.
For A prefix: `pref=np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(w))); A=pref[n:n+L?]?` A_i = pref[i+n]-pref[i]. i 0..n-1. So `A = pref[n:2*n] - pref[0:n]`? pref length L+1=2n. pref[n:2*n] length n indices n..2n-1, pref[0:n] 0..n-1. For i=n-1: pref[2n-1]-pref[n-1] sum w[n-1..2n-2], correct. Good.

def run_dense_lbfgs(h,n,deadline):
   best_h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=eval_fast(best_h,n)
   bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*n
   # schedule: list of (alpha, mu, bin_lamb)
   schedules = [
      (50.0, 1e-2, 0.0),
      (200.0, 1e-3, 0.0),
      (1000.0, 1e-4, 0.0),
      (5000.0, 1e-4, 0.0),
      (20000.0, 1e-5, 0.0),
      (50, 1e-2, 1e-3), # binary push
      (200, 1e-3, 1e-3),
      (1000, 1e-4, 1e-4),
   ]
   # but too many calls. Use for loop until deadline.
   for alpha,mu,lamb in schedules:
      if time.time()>deadline-30: break
      _CALLBACK_DEADLINE = deadline - 5
      maxit = 5000
      try minimize(...)
      accept if c_new < best_c - 1e-12
   # optionally repeat cycle with slightly perturbed h? No.
```
We should not make a single minimize run to entire phase; callback at phase deadline cannot be interrupted before next schedule. Better set per-call deadline smaller: e.g., each call has `call_deadline = min(deadline, time.time()+15)`? If each minimize call limited 10-30s, we can try multiple alphas. But if a difficult high-alpha call needs more than 15s to converge, breaking into repeated calls from updated hcontinues with same alpha. We can continue same alpha if callback stopped and improved. Simpler: for each schedule, call multiple times until no improvement or time.

Pseudo:
```
schedules = [
  (20,0.01,0.0), (100,0.003,0.0), (500,0.001,0.0), (2000,0.0005,0.0),
  (10000,0.0002,0.0), (20,0.01,0.002), (100,0.003,0.002), (500,0.001,0.001)
]
for sched in schedules:
  while time.time() < deadline - 20:
      call_deadline = min(deadline-5, time.time()+call_s)
      res=minimize(... callback stops at call_deadline)
      hnew=project...
      cnew=eval...
      improved = cnew < best_c - 1e-13
      if improved: best_c=...
      elif no improvement and perhaps callback not reached? break schedule
       # If callback reached but no improve, break after one no-improve for this schedule.
```

For each candidate, we can compute delta in scalar loops over A (A ~20) to avoid numpy call overhead? Python scalar loop A=20, pool=64 =>1280 ops per iter; 1000=1.28M, very fast. Let's implement scalar loop for active delta:
```
def candidate_delta_active(i,j,h,n,active_idx):
  deltas=[]
  for idx in active_idx:
    k=idx-(n-1); d=0.0
    m=i-k
    if 0<=m<n: d -= 1.0 - h[m]
    m=j-k
    if 0<=m<n: d += 1.0 - h[m]
    s=i+k
    if 0<=s<n: d += h[s]
    s=j+k
    if 0<=s<n: d -= h[s]
    if k==0: d -=2.0
    if k==i-j: d +=1.0
    if k==j-i: d +=1.0
    deltas.append(d)
  return array
```

`project_feasible` for arbitrary h:
Need ensure sum exactly target within floating. Use water-filling:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
   target=0.5*n
   h=np.clip(h,0,1).astype(float)
   for _ in range(10000):
      s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      if diff>0:
         room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
         if tot<1e-13: break
         step=diff/tot
         if step>=1: h += step*room
         else: h += step*room
      else:
         tot=h.sum()
         if tot<1e-13: break
         step=diff/tot
         if step<=-1: h += step*tot? Actually scale h by target/tot? h *= target/tot
         else: h += step*h
   return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
If diff negative and step <=-1, `h *= target/tot`; if step > -1, `h += step*h` reduces all by fraction. This may not exact if clipping nonzero? If no clipping, exact. If clipping, loop.

# find index with room if diff>0 and h<1 else with h>0
if abs(diff)>1e-12:
  if diff>0:
    idx=np.argmax(1-h); h[idx]+=diff if h[idx]+diff<=1 else ...
  else: idx=np.argmax(h); h[idx]+=diff

def full_c_counts_fft(h,n):

Check sliding_corr slice:
```
def sliding_corr(w, arr):
    L=len(w); n=len(arr)
    size=next_power_two(L+n-1)
    f1=np.rfft(w,size); f2=np.rfft(arr[::-1],size)
    out=np.irfft(f1*f2,size)
    return out[n-1:n+n]
```
Length n. Good.

def run_dense_lbfgs(h,n,deadline):
    best_h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_c=eval_c_internal(best_h,n)

    elif n <= 1600: call_s=15

       (20, 1e-2, 0.0),
       (100, 3e-3, 0.0),
       (500, 1e-3, 0.0),
       (2000, 5e-4, 0.0),

             ...

`binary_swap_search` algorithm in more concrete terms:
```
def binary_swap_search(h,n,deadline):
   hb=make_binary(project_feasible(h,n), n)
   if n even and hb.sum()!=n/2: fix
   counts=full_c_counts_direct(hb,n)
   counts=np.round(counts).astype(float) if close else counts
   M=float(counts.max())
   best_h=hb.copy(); best_counts=counts.copy(); bestM=M
   # maintain ones/zeros as arrays? We'll recompute from hb each iteration for simplicity? n=2048, okay.
   no_improve=0
   while time.time()<deadline-5:
      M=counts.max()
      # active: counts >= M - 1e-6; if > 50 top 50
      active=np.where(counts >= M - 1e-6)[0]
      if len(active)>50:
         order=np.argsort(-counts[active]); active=active[order[:50]]
      if len(active)==0: break
      # compute gradient
      grad=np.zeros(n)
      inv=1.0/len(active)
      for idx in active:
         k=idx-(n-1)
         wm=inv
         if k>=0:
            nv=n-k
            grad[k:n] += wm*(1.0 - hb[0:nv])
            grad[0:nv] += -wm*hb[k:n]
         else:
            kk=-k; nv=n-kk
            grad[0:nv] += wm*(1.0 - hb[kk:n])
            grad[kk:n] += -wm*hb[0:nv]
      ones=np.where(hb>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.where(hb<=0.5)[0]
      if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
      pos_grad=grad[ones]; neg_grad=grad[zeros]
      # top lists
      pos_order=np.argsort(-pos_grad); pos=ones[pos_order[:10]] if len...
      neg_order=np.argsort(neg_grad); neg=zeros[neg_order[:10]]
      # if no pos>0 or neg<0 use random top
      candidates=[]
      for i in pos[:12]:
         for j in neg[:12]:
            if i!=j: candidates.append((i,j))
      # add random
      for _ in range(20): candidates.append((rg.choice(ones), rg.choice(zeros)))
      # evaluate active estimates
      best_est=None; best_cand=None; top_list=[]
      for (i,j) in candidates:
         deltas = candidate_delta_active_scalar(i,j,hb,n,active)
         est = float(np.max(counts[active] + deltas))
         # primary score = est; secondary = sum active
         score = est
         if best is None or score < best_est: ...
         # collect candidates with est < M
         if est < M - 1e-6: top_list append (est,i,j)
      # If no top_list, maybe try top 10 by est <= M+0.5
      if no top_list: select candidates with est < M + 0.5 up to 5
      else: top_list sort by est
      accepted=False
      for est,i,j in top_list[:5]:
          delta=swap_delta_counts(hb,i,j,n)
          new_counts=counts+delta
          # clean
          newM = float(new_counts.max())
          # secondary: if newM < M, accept. else if newM==M and sum largest 5 decreases maybe.
          if newM < M - 1e-6:
              accept
          elif abs(newM-M)<=1e-6:
              # compute sort top? maybe skip
          else: reject
      if accepted: update M, no_improve=0
      else:
         no_improve+=1
         if no_improve>80:
             # random perturbation: perform up to 20 random swaps that do not increase M by >1, or reset active slack? Use temperature.
             if random acceptance fails: break
```
To avoid missing improvements due to active truncation, when active len >50, gradient not accurate. But if many max lags, hard. Could choose active top 80. For n=2048, A=80, candidate pool 144, scalar loops 11k per iter; okay. Use A=40.

Detailed binary swap:

def binary_swap_search(h,n,deadline,seed=1):
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  hb=make_binary(project_feasible(h,n),n)
  # ensure count exact: make_binary does.
  counts=full_c_counts_direct(hb,n)
  counts=round_if_close(counts,n)
  M=float(counts.max())
  best_h=hb.copy(); best_counts=counts.copy(); bestM=M
  no_improve=0
  while time.time() < deadline - 5:
     counts=round_if_close(counts,n)
     M=float(counts.max())
     if M < bestM - 1e-7:
        bestM=M; best_h=hb.copy(); best_counts=counts.copy()
     active = np.where(counts >= M - 1e-6)[0]
     if len(active) > 50:
        idxorder=np.argsort(-counts[active])
        active=active[idxorder[:50]]
     if len(active)==0: break
     grad=np.zeros(n)
     inv=1.0/len(active)
     for idx in active: ...
     ones=np.where(hb > 0.5)[0]
     zeros=np.where(hb <= 0.5)[0]
     if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
     pos=ones[np.argsort(-grad[ones])[:12]]
     neg=zeros[np.argsort(grad[zeros])[:12]] # most negative
     # if grad values no sign, use random
     cands=[]
     for i in pos:
       for j in neg:
         if i!=j: cands.append((int(i),int(j)))
     # add random maybe 16
     for _ in range(16):
       i=holes? one = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))], j=zeros[...]
       if i!=j: cands.append
     # Evaluate top candidates
     # To reduce work, evaluate first 60 cands
     best_est = 1e100; top=[]
     for cand in cands[:80]:
        i,j=cand
        deltas = candidate_delta_active(i,j,hb,n,active)
        est = float(np.max(counts[active] + deltas))
        # if est < M + 0.5 keep
        if est < M + 0.5:
           if len(top)<10 or est < best_est: top append sort manually
     # sort top by est
     top.sort()
     accepted=False
     for est,i,j in top[:5]:
        delta=swap_delta_counts(hb,i,j,n)
        new_counts=counts+delta
        new_counts=round_if_close(new_counts,n)
        newM=float(np.max(new_counts))
        if newM < M - 1e-6:
           accept
        else:
           # secondary if equal M
           # compute sum active_slack old/new
           if abs(newM-M)<=1e-6:
              old_sec = float(np.sum(counts[np.argsort(-counts)[:20]]))
              new_sec = float(np.sum(new_counts[np.argsort(-new_counts)[:20]]))
              if new_sec < old_sec - 1e-5: accept
     if accepted:
        hb[i]=0.0; hb[j]=1.0
        counts=new_counts
        no_improve=0
     else:
        no_improve+=1
        if no_improve >= 60:
           # random anneal: try 50 random swaps accepting if newM <= M+2 maybe and sometimes sec lower
           accepted_any=False
           for _ in range(80):
             i=ones..., j=zeros...
             delta=swap_delta_counts(...)
             new_counts=counts+delta; newM=max
             if newM <= M + 2 + 0.1*no_improve/10:
                # accept with probability 0.2 if worse, 0.8 if same
                if newM < M or (abs(newM-M)<=1e-6 and rng.rand()<0.5) or (newM > M and rng.rand()<0.05):
                   apply, accepted_any=True; break
           if not accepted_any:
              break
             else:
               no_improve=0
  # final update best
  if bestM < M: ...
  c=bestM*2.0/n
  return best_h, c
```
Need be careful: `top.sort()` with tuples `(est,i,j)` okay. `cands` list of tuples. No lambdas.

`round_if_close`:
```
def round_if_close(counts,n):
   rounded=np.round(counts)
   if np.all(np.abs(counts-rounded) < 1e-4): return rounded
   return np.clip(counts,0.0,float(n))
```
For n=2048, 1e-4 okay.

`candidate_delta_active` scalar returns numpy array:
```
def candidate_delta_active(i,j,hb,n,active):
    deltas = np.empty(len(active))
    for t in range(len(active)):
       idx=active[t]; k=idx-(n-1); d=0.0
       m=i-k
       if 0 <= m < n: d -= 1.0 - hb[m]
       m=j-k
       if 0 <= m < n: d += 1.0 - hb[m]
       s=i+k
       if 0 <= s < n: d += hb[s]
       s=j+k
       if 0 <= s < n: d -= hb[s]
       if k == 0: d -= 2.0
       if k == i-j: d += 1.0
       if k == j-i: d += 1.0
       deltas[t]=d
    return deltas
```
Since hb binary float. Corrections for i-j and j-i within active. Good.

`swap_delta_counts` vectorized. Need ensure i,j ints. For h binary but formula works generally? Derivation corrections assume h_i=1,h_j=0? We used old values in slices; corrections derived for binary swap? Let's check if h fractional? We only call for binary. Good.
Slices use `hb` old. We should store `old_rev = (1.0 - hb)[::-1]` and `hb` before swap. Then:
```
def swap_delta_counts(hb,i,j,n):
    L=2*n-1
    delta=np.zeros(L)
    rev=(1.0-hb)[::-1]
    delta[i:i+n] -= rev  # since n+i exclusive, i+n
    delta[j:j+n] += rev
    delta[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] += hb
    delta[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] -= hb
    delta[n-1] -= 2.0
    l1=n-1 + (i-j)
    if 0 <= l1 < L: delta[l1] += 1.0
    l2=n-1 + (j-i)
    if 0 <= l2 < L: delta[l2] += 1.0
    return delta
```
Check slice source i: earlier `delta[i:n+i]` (i to i+n-1). `delta[i:i+n]` correct length n. Source j similarly. Target slice `n-1-i` to `2*n-1-i` exclusive length n. Good.

After applying, count maybe out of [0,n] by floating 1e-8; round.

In binary search, candidate delta active should match full delta for those lags. For binary, yes. Need ensure corrections at k=0 and cross for full match active. For n=2 tested.

Candidate delta active if active includes k=0? For binary k=0 count always 0, delta should 0. Formula: source_i - (1-h_i)=0; source_j +(1-h_j)=1; target_i +h_i=1; target_j -h_j=0; corrections k0 -2; cross if? i-j not0. total0. Good.

Now, if candidate i,j are adjacent positions with i-j = -? corrections add at lag maybe active. Good.

Potential bug: The source_j correction for target j self: source_j + (1-h_j)=1, target_j -h_j=0, correction k0 -2? Wait k0 corrections total -2 combines source_i target_i and source_j target_j. For k0, source_i removal 0, source_j add1, target_i add1, target_j 0, total2; corrections -2 ->0. Good.
For i,j cross corrections +1 each. What about source_i target_j raw: source_i removal when m=j: -(1-h_j)=-1; target_j removal when s=i: -h_i=-1; total -2; correction +1 -> -1. Good. source_j target i raw 0+0+1 -> +1. Good. All terms with only one changed accounted.

`resize_array`:
```
def resize_array(arr,n):
   m=len(arr)
   if m==n: return arr.copy()
   old=np.linspace(0.0,m-1.0,m)
   new=np.linspace(0.0,n-1.0,n)
   return np.interp(new, old, arr)
```

`random_binary`:
```
def random_binary(n,rng):
 h=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,size=n//2,replace=False); h[idx]=1; return h

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+budget_s-10 if budget_s>100 else t0+budget_s-5
    if deadline < t0+30: deadline=t0+budget_s-1
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
        parent=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).ravel()
        if parent.size==0: parent=None
    except Exception:
        parent=None
    best_h=None; best_c=float('inf'); best_n=0
    # target ns:
    ns=[]
    n_parent=0
    if parent is not None:
       n_parent=parent.size
       if n_parent %2 !=0: n_parent +=1
       if 64 <= n_parent <= 4096: ns.append(n_parent)
    for m in [512,1024,2048,4096]:
       if m not in ns: ns.append(m)
    # sort ns by maybe parent first, then size? np.argsort(ns)
    # limit ns by time: quick_deadline = t0+120
    phase_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 130.0)
    cs=[]; ns_list=[]; hs=[]
    for n in ns:
       if time.time() > phase_deadline - 20: break
       if n < 64 or n > 4096: continue
       # maybe skip 4096 if n>2048 and phasedeadline close
       starts = quick_starts(n, rng, parent)
       # limit starts to 12
       for h in starts[:12]:
           h=project_feasible(h,n)
           c=eval_c_exact(h,n)
           if c < best_c:
              best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n
           cs.append(c); ns_list.append(n); hs.append(h)
       if time.time() > phase_deadline: break
    if best_h is None:
       n=512
       best_h=thue_morse_start? or poly; project; best_c=eval_c_exact
    # select top configs for dense
    if len(cs)>0:
       order=np.argsort(cs)
    else: order=np.array([],int)
    selected=[]; count_per_n={}
    # Need iterate order and get hs.
    for pos in order[:50]:
       n=ns_list[pos]
       cnt=count_per_n.get(n,0)
       if cnt < 4:
          selected.append((hs[pos], n)); count_per_n[n]=cnt+1
       if len(selected) >= 10: break
    # Dense phase
    dense_deadline = max(time.time(), min(deadline - 180, t0 + 850)) # leave 180 for binary/final
    # allocate based on selected order (already sorted by c)
    for idx,(h,n) in enumerate(selected):
       if time.time() > dense_deadline - 20: break
       # time per config depending on n and index
       if n <= 600: rem=70
       elif n <= 1500: rem=80
       else: rem=100
       if idx >= 4: rem = int(rem*0.6)
       conf_deadline = min(dense_deadline, time.time() + float(rem))
       if conf_deadline > time.time() + 20:
          hnew,cnew=run_dense_lbfgs(h,n,conf_deadline)
          if cnew < best_c:
             best_c=cnew; best_h=hnew.copy(); best_n=n
    # Maybe if selected empty, run from best
    # Binary phase
    binary_deadline = min(deadline - 80, t0 + budget_s - 120) # hmm
    if time.time() < deadline - 100:
       # top candidates: best_h and maybe first selected
       b1_deadline = min(deadline - 80, time.time() + 100)
       hb,cb = binary_swap_search(best_h, best_n, b1_deadline, seed=int(seed)+1)
       if cb < best_c: best...
       if time.time() < deadline - 80 and len(selected)>0:
          hb,cb = binary_swap_search(selected[0][0], selected[0][1], min(deadline-80, time.time()+50), seed+2)
    # Final dense polish from best_h with remaining
    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
       # run dense with smaller allocation from best_h, maybe also binary best?
       hpol, cpol = run_dense_lbfgs(best_h,best_n,deadline-5)
       if cpol < best_c: best...
    # final projection and eval
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=eval_c_exact(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, best_n
```

def poly_start(n, deg, a,b,c,d):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    if deg==3: y=np.mod(a*i*i*i + b*i*i + c*i + d, 1.0)
    else: y=np.mod(a*i*i + b*i + c, 1.0)
    h=np.zeros(n); h[np.argsort(y)[:n//2]]=1.0
    return h
```

def thue_morse_start(n):
   try: p=np.bit_count(np.arange(n, dtype=np.uint64))
   except Exception: p=np.array([bin(i).count('1') for i in range(n)], dtype=int)
   h=(1.0 - ((p & 1).astype(float)))
   if np.sum(h) != n//2:
      return make_binary(h,n)
   return h
```

Let's write `run_dense_lbfgs` in code with schedule. Need ensure `_CALLBACK_DEADLINE` set before each `minimize`. If `minimize` raises because initial infeasible? h feasible.

```
def run_dense_lbfgs(h, n, deadline):
    global _CALLBACK_DEADLINE
    if n < 1: return h, 0
    best_h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_c=eval_c_exact(best_h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] * n
    if n <= 600: call_s=25.0
    elif n <= 1500: call_s=18.0
    elif n <= 3000: call_s=12.0
    else: call_s=8.0
    schedules = [...]
    for alpha,mu,lamb in schedules:
        attempts=0; no_improve=0
        while time.time() < deadline - 15 and attempts < 6 and no_improve < 2:
            call_end = time.time() + call_s
            if call_end > deadline - 5: call_end = deadline - 5
            if time.time() >= call_end: break
            _CALLBACK_DEADLINE = call_end
            try:
                res = minimize(dense_obj, best_h, jac=dense_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu,lamb), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=..., callback=time_callback)
            except Exception:
                no_improve +=1; break
            attempts +=1
            if getattr(res, 'success', False) or res.x is not None:
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
                if x.shape[0] != n: x = best_h
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                    no_improve +=1; continue
                x = project_feasible(x,n)
                c = eval_c_exact(x,n)
                if c < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_h=x; best_c=c; no_improve=0
                else:
                    no_improve +=1
            else:
                no_improve +=1
        # after schedule, if time no break outer
    return best_h, best_c
```
If a call improves, attempts reset? We reset no_improve but attempts continues up to 6. Could do more if improvements. Maybe okay. If first schedule low alpha takes many improvements but consumes time, later high alpha maybe not. To ensure progression, set `attempts` max 3 maybe. Use 3. Low alpha likely not too important. Use 3. Schedules 10 =>30 calls max. Good.

Set `options={'maxiter':50000,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':500000,'maxls':50,'maxcor':50}`. The callback stops; high maxfun not problem.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(10000):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            frac = diff / tot
            h += frac * room
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            frac = diff / tot
            h += frac * h
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(h.sum())
    diff = target - s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
            h[idx] -= sub
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
```
If add doesn't cover diff due one element not enough, sum still off. But water should. Could loop final a few:

for _ in range(50): ...
```

def eval_c_exact(h,n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)
```

def round_if_close(counts, n):

   if np.all(np.abs(counts-rounded) < 1e-4): return rounded
   return np.clip(counts, 0.0, float(n))

`full_c_counts_fft`:
```
def full_c_counts_fft(h,n):
    size=next_power_two(2*n-1)
    fh=np.rfft(h, size)
    fv=np.rfft((1.0 - h)[::-1], size)
    out=np.irfft(fh*fv, size)[:2*n-1]
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out
```
If n=0? no.

`smooth_max_count`: If c empty? no.
```
def smooth_max_count(c, alpha):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if alpha <= 0: return m
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    zmax=float(np.max(z))
    if not math.isfinite(zmax): return m
    e=np.exp(z-zmax)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0 or not math.isfinite(s): return m
    return m + (zmax + math.log(s)) / alpha
```
`math.log(s)`.

`dense_obj`:
```
def dense_obj(x,n,alpha,mu,lamb):
    c=full_c_counts_fft(x,n)
    val=smooth_max_count(c, alpha)
    s=float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5*n
    val += mu*s*s
    if lamb != 0.0:
        val += lamb * float(np.sum(x*(1.0-x)))
    return float(val)
```

`sliding_corr`:
```
def sliding_corr(w, arr):
    L=len(w); m=len(arr)
    size=next_power_two(L + m - 1)
    f1=np.rfft(w, size)
    f2=np.rfft(arr[::-1], size)
    out=np.irfft(f1*f2, size)
    return out[m-1:m+m]
```
Note if m=0 no.

`dense_grad`:
```
def dense_grad(x,n,alpha,mu,lamb):
    c=full_c_counts_fft(x,n)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    zmax=float(np.max(z))
    e=np.exp(z-zmax)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if s <=0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        w=np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w=e/s
    L=2*n-1
    pref=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w)))
    A=pref[n:2*n] - pref[0:n]
    hr=x[::-1]
    B=sliding_corr(w, hr)
    C=sliding_corr(w[::-1], hr)
    grad=A - B - C
    ssum=float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5*n
    if mu !=0: grad += 2*mu*ssum
    if lamb !=0: grad += lamb*(1.0 - 2.0*x)
    return grad
```
Check `pref[n:2*n]`: pref length L+1=2n. Slice n:2n length n. okay.

Could optimize if `alpha` small? no.

def quick_starts(n, rng, parent):
    starts=[]
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
       r=resize_array(parent,n)
       starts.append(project_feasible(r,n))

       starts.append(tm)
       starts.append(project_feasible(1.0-tm,n))
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    coeffs=[(SQRT2, SQRT3, 0.0), (SQRT3, SQRT5, 0.37), (PHI, SQRT2, 0.11), (1.234, SQRT3, 0.567)]
    for a,b,c in coeffs:
       y=np.mod(a*i*i + b*i + c, 1.0)
       h=np.zeros(n); h[np.argsort(y)[:n//2]]=1.0
       starts.append(h)
       starts.append(1.0-h)
    # cubic two
    y=np.mod(1.234*i*i*i + SQRT2*i*i + SQRT3*i + 0.25, 1.0)
    h=np.zeros(n); h[np.argsort(y)[:n//2]]=1.0
    starts.append(h); starts.append(1-h)
    # beatty
    for alpha in (0.414,0.618):
       starts.append(beatty_start(n, alpha))
    starts.append(random_binary(n, rng))
    return starts
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_CALLBACK_DEADLINE = 0.0
SQRT2 = math.sqrt(2.0)
SQRT3 = math.sqrt(3.0)
SQRT5 = math.sqrt(5.0)
PHI = (1.0 + SQRT5) / 2.0


def time_callback(xk):
    return time.time() > _CALLBACK_DEADLINE


def next_power_two(m):
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def is_power_two(n):
    n = int(n)
    return n > 0 and (n & (n - 1)) == 0


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(10000):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    s = float(h.sum())
    diff = target - s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        for _ in range(100):
            if diff > 0:
                idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
                if add <= 1e-15:
                    break
                h[idx] += add
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmax(h))
                sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
                if sub <= 1e-15:
                    break
                h[idx] -= sub
            s = float(h.sum())
            diff = target - s
            if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
                break
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def make_binary(h, n):
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    order = np.argsort(-np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    k = n // 2
    if k > 0:
        b[order[:k]] = 1.0
    if n % 2 == 1 and k < n:
        b[order[k]] = 0.5
    return b


def eval_c_exact(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return m * dx


def full_c_counts_fft(h, n):
    size = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    fh = np.rfft(h, size)
    fv = np.rfft((1.0 - h)[::-1], size)
    out = np.irfft(fh * fv, size)[:2 * n - 1]
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def smooth_max_count(c, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        return m
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    if not math.isfinite(zmax):
        return m
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return m
    return m + (zmax + math.log(s)) / alpha


def dense_obj(x, n, alpha, mu, lamb):
    c = full_c_counts_fft(x, n)
    val = smooth_max_count(c, alpha)
    s = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
    val += mu * s * s
    if lamb != 0.0:
        val += lamb * float(np.sum(x * (1.0 - x)))
    return float(val)


def sliding_corr_with_f2(w, f_arr_rev, size, n):
    f1 = np.rfft(w, size)
    out = np.irfft(f1 * f_arr_rev, size)
    return out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def dense_grad(x, n, alpha, mu, lamb):
    c = full_c_counts_fft(x, n)
    m = float(np.max(c))

    if alpha > 0.0:
        z = alpha * (c - m)
        zmax = float(np.max(z))
        e = np.exp(z - zmax)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = e / s
    else:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0

    size = next_power_two(3 * n - 2)
    f_x = np.rfft(x, size)

    pref = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w)))
    A = pref[n:2 * n] - pref[0:n]

    B = sliding_corr_with_f2(w, f_x, size, n)
    C = sliding_corr_with_f2(w[::-1], f_x, size, n)

    grad = A - B - C

    ssum = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
    if mu != 0.0:
        grad += 2.0 * mu * ssum
    if lamb != 0.0:
        grad += lamb * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
    return grad


def resize_array(arr, n):
    m = int(arr.size)
    if m == n:
        return np.asarray(arr, dtype=float).copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1.0, m)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(new, old, np.asarray(arr, dtype=float))


def thue_morse_start(n):
    try:
        pc = np.bit_count(np.arange(n, dtype=np.uint64))
    except Exception:
        pc = np.array([bin(i).count("1") for i in range(n)], dtype=int)
    h = 1.0 - (pc & 1).astype(float)
    s = float(h.sum())
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    if abs(s - target) > 1e-12:
        h = make_binary(h, n)
    return h


def beatty_start(n, alpha):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    target_ones = n // 2
    cnt = 0
    for k in range(1, 10 * n + 5):
        pos = int((k * alpha) % n)
        if h[pos] == 0.0:
            h[pos] = 1.0
            cnt += 1
            if cnt == target_ones:
                break
    if cnt < target_ones:
        zeros = np.where(h == 0.0)[0]
        for idx in zeros[:target_ones - cnt]:
            h[idx] = 1.0
    if n % 2 == 1:
        zeros = np.where(h == 0.0)[0]
        if len(zeros) > 0:
            h[zeros[0]] = 0.5
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def random_binary(n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    k = n // 2
    if k > 0:
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    if n % 2 == 1:
        zeros = np.where(h == 0.0)[0]
        if len(zeros) > 0:
            h[zeros[0]] = 0.5
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def quadratic_start(n, a, b, c):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    y = np.mod(a * i * i + b * i + c, 1.0)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    h[np.argsort(y)[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return h


def cubic_start(n, a, b, c, d):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    y = np.mod(a * i * i * i + b * i * i + c * i + d, 1.0)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    h[np.argsort(y)[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return h


def quick_starts(n, rng, parent):
    starts = []

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        r = resize_array(parent, n)
        starts.append(project_feasible(r, n))
        starts.append(project_feasible(make_binary(r, n), n))

    if is_power_two(n):
        tm = thue_morse_start(n)
        starts.append(project_feasible(tm, n))
        starts.append(project_feasible(1.0 - tm, n))

    qcoefs = [
        (SQRT2, SQRT3, 0.0),
        (SQRT3, SQRT5, 0.37),
        (PHI, SQRT2, 0.11),
        (1.234, SQRT3, 0.567),
    ]
    for a, b, c in qcoefs:
        h = quadratic_start(n, a, b, c)
        starts.append(project_feasible(h, n))
        starts.append(project_feasible(1.0 - h, n))

    for a, b, c, d in [(1.234, SQRT2, SQRT3, 0.25), (PHI, SQRT3, 1.777, 0.53)]:
        h = cubic_start(n, a, b, c, d)
        starts.append(project_feasible(h, n))
        starts.append(project_feasible(1.0 - h, n))

    for alpha in (0.414, 0.618):
        starts.append(beatty_start(n, alpha))

    starts.append(random_binary(n, rng))
    return starts


def run_dense_lbfgs(h, n, deadline):
    global _CALLBACK_DEADLINE

    best_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = eval_c_exact(best_h, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    if n <= 640:
        call_s = 25.0
    elif n <= 1280:
        call_s = 18.0
    elif n <= 2560:
        call_s = 12.0
    else:
        call_s = 8.0

    schedules = [
        (20.0, 1e-2, 0.0),
        (100.0, 3e-3, 0.0),
        (500.0, 1e-3, 0.0),
        (2000.0, 5e-4, 0.0),
        (10000.0, 2e-4, 0.0),
        (50000.0, 1e-4, 0.0),
        (100.0, 3e-3, 1e-4),
        (1000.0, 1e-3, 2e-4),
        (5000.0, 5e-4, 5e-5),
    ]

    for alpha, mu, lamb in schedules:
        attempts = 0
        no_improve = 0
        while time.time() < deadline - 15 and attempts < 3 and no_improve < 2:
            call_end = time.time() + call_s
            if call_end > deadline - 5:
                call_end = deadline - 5
            if time.time() >= call_end:
                break
            _CALLBACK_DEADLINE = call_end
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    dense_obj,
                    best_h,
                    jac=dense_grad,
                    args=(n, alpha, mu, lamb),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 50000,
                        "ftol": 1e-18,
                        "gtol": 1e-12,
                        "maxfun": 500000,
                        "maxls": 50,
                        "maxcor": 50,
                    },
                    callback=time_callback,
                )
            except Exception:
                no_improve += 1
                break

            attempts += 1
            x = getattr(res, "x", None)
            if x is None:
                no_improve += 1
                continue
            x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
            if x.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                no_improve += 1
                continue

            x = project_feasible(x, n)
            c = eval_c_exact(x, n)
            if c < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = x
                best_c = c
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

    return best_h, best_c


def round_if_close(counts, n):
    rounded = np.round(counts)
    if np.all(np.abs(counts - rounded) < 1e-4):
        return rounded
    return np.clip(counts, 0.0, float(n))


def candidate_delta_active(i, j, hb, n, active):
    deltas = np.empty(len(active), dtype=float)
    imj = int(i) - int(j)
    jim = int(j) - int(i)
    for t in range(len(active)):
        idx = int(active[t])
        k = idx - (n - 1)
        d = 0.0

        m = i - k
        if 0 <= m < n:
            d -= 1.0 - hb[m]

        m = j - k
        if 0 <= m < n:
            d += 1.0 - hb[m]

        s = i + k
        if 0 <= s < n:
            d += hb[s]

        s = j + k
        if 0 <= s < n:
            d -= hb[s]

        if k == 0:
            d -= 2.0
        if k == imj:
            d += 1.0
        if k == jim:
            d += 1.0
        deltas[t] = d
    return deltas


def swap_delta_counts(hb, i, j, n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    rev = (1.0 - hb)[::-1]

    delta[i:i + n] -= rev
    delta[j:j + n] += rev

    delta[n - 1 - i:2 * n - 1 - i] += hb
    delta[n - 1 - j:2 * n - 1 - j] -= hb

    delta[n - 1] -= 2.0

    l1 = n - 1 + (i - j)
    if 0 <= l1 < L:
        delta[l1] += 1.0

    l2 = n - 1 + (j - i)
    if 0 <= l2 < L:
        delta[l2] += 1.0

    return delta


def binary_swap_search(h, n, deadline, seed=1):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    hb = project_feasible(h, n)
    hb = make_binary(hb, n)

    counts = full_c_counts_fft(hb, n)
    counts = round_if_close(counts, n)
    M = float(np.max(counts))

    best_h = hb.copy()
    best_counts = counts.copy()
    best_M = M

    no_improve = 0

    while time.time() < deadline - 5:
        counts = round_if_close(counts, n)
        M = float(np.max(counts))
        if M < best_M - 1e-7:
            best_M = M
            best_h = hb.copy()
            best_counts = counts.copy()

        active = np.where(counts >= M - 1e-6)[0]
        if len(active) > 50:
            ordact = np.argsort(-counts[active])
            active = active[ordact[:50]]
        if len(active) == 0:
            break

        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        invw = 1.0 / float(len(active))
        for idx in active:
            k = int(idx) - (n - 1)
            wm = invw
            if k >= 0:
                nv = n - k
                grad[k:n] += wm * (1.0 - hb[0:nv])
                grad[0:nv] += -wm * hb[k:n]
            else:
                kk = -k
                nv = n - kk
                grad[0:nv] += wm * (1.0 - hb[kk:n])
                grad[kk:n] += -wm * hb[0:nv]

        ones = np.where(hb > 0.5)[0]
        zeros = np.where(hb <= 0.5)[0]
        if len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0:
            break

        pos_order = np.argsort(-grad[ones])
        neg_order = np.argsort(grad[zeros])
        pos = ones[pos_order[:12] if len(ones) >= 12 else len(ones)]
        neg = zeros[neg_order[:12] if len(zeros) >= 12 else len(zeros)]

        cands = []
        for pi in pos:
            for qj in neg:
                if int(pi) != int(qj):
                    cands.append((int(pi), int(qj)))

        for _ in range(16):
            ii = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
            jj = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
            if ii != jj:
                cands.append((ii, jj))

        top = []
        best_est = 1e100
        for ii, jj in cands[:80]:
            deltas = candidate_delta_active(ii, jj, hb, n, active)
            est = float(np.max(counts[active] + deltas))
            if est < M + 0.5:
                if len(top) < 10 or est < best_est:
                    top.append((est, ii, jj))
                    if est < best_est:
                        best_est = est
                    top.sort()
                    if len(top) == 10 and top[-1][0] > best_est:
                        top.pop()

        old_sec = float(np.sum(counts[active]))
        accepted = False
        accept_i = -1
        accept_j = -1
        accept_counts = None
        accept_M = M

        if len(top) > 0:
            for est, ii, jj in top[:5]:
                d = swap_delta_counts(hb, ii, jj, n)
                nc = counts + d
                nc = round_if_close(nc, n)
                nM = float(np.max(nc))
                nsec = float(np.sum(nc[active]))
                if nM < M - 1e-7:
                    accepted = True
                    accept_i = ii
                    accept_j = jj
                    accept_counts = nc
                    accept_M = nM
                    break
                if abs(nM - M) <= 1e-7 and nsec < old_sec - 1e-5:
                    accepted = True
                    accept_i = ii
                    accept_j = jj
                    accept_counts = nc
                    accept_M = nM
                    break

        if accepted:
            hb[accept_i] = 0.0
            hb[accept_j] = 1.0
            counts = accept_counts
            M = accept_M
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 80:
                break

    counts = round_if_close(counts, n)
    M = float(np.max(counts))
    if M < best_M - 1e-9:
        best_M = M
        best_h = hb.copy()
    c = best_M * (2.0 / float(n))
    return project_feasible(best_h, n), c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    avail = float(budget_s)
    if avail > 100.0:
        margin = 10.0
    elif avail > 30.0:
        margin = 5.0
    else:
        margin = 1.0
    deadline = t0 + avail - margin
    if deadline <= t0:
        deadline = t0 + max(1.0, avail)

    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        if parent.size == 0:
            parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    ns = []
    if parent is not None:
        nparent = int(parent.size)
        if nparent % 2 != 0:
            nparent += 1
        if 64 <= nparent <= 4096:
            ns.append(nparent)
    for m in (512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
        if m not in ns:
            ns.append(m)

    quick_end = min(deadline, t0 + 130.0)

    cs = []
    ns_list = []
    hs = []

    if parent is not None:
        hp = project_feasible(parent, int(parent.size))
        c0 = eval_c_exact(hp, int(parent.size))
        if math.isfinite(c0):
            best_h = hp.copy()
            best_c = c0
            best_n = int(parent.size)

    for n in ns:
        if time.time() > quick_end - 15:
            break
        if n < 64 or n > 4096:
            continue
        stencil = quick_starts(n, rng, parent)
        max_starts = 10 if n <= 1024 else 6
        for h in stencil[:max_starts]:
            if time.time() > quick_end - 5:
                break
            try:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                c = eval_c_exact(h, n)
            except Exception:
                continue
            if not math.isfinite(c):
                continue
            cs.append(c)
            ns_list.append(int(n))
            hs.append(h)
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_n = int(n)

    if best_h is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_h)):
        fallback_n = 512
        best_h = project_feasible(thue_morse_start(fallback_n), fallback_n)
        best_c = eval_c_exact(best_h, fallback_n)
        best_n = fallback_n
        if len(cs) == 0:
            cs.append(best_c)
            ns_list.append(fallback_n)
            hs.append(best_h)

    if len(cs) == 0:
        order = np.array([], dtype=int)
    else:
        order = np.argsort(cs)

    selected = []
    count_per_n = {}
    for pos in order:
        n = ns_list[pos]
        cur = count_per_n.get(n, 0)
        if cur < 4:
            selected.append((hs[pos], int(n)))
            count_per_n[n] = cur + 1
        if len(selected) >= 12:
            break

    if not selected:
        selected = [(best_h, best_n)]

    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
    dense_end = time.time() + 0.60 * remaining

    for idx, (h, n) in enumerate(selected):
        if time.time() > dense_end - 20:
            break
        if n <= 700:
            rem = 75.0
        elif n <= 1500:
            rem = 85.0
        elif n <= 2600:
            rem = 105.0
        else:
            rem = 90.0
        if idx >= 5:
            rem *= 0.55
        conf_end = min(dense_end, time.time() + rem)
        if conf_end <= time.time() + 20:
            continue
        try:
            h2, c2 = run_dense_lbfgs(h, n, conf_end)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2.copy()
                best_c = c2
                best_n = int(n)
        except Exception:
            pass

    remaining2 = max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
    binary_end = time.time() + 0.40 * remaining2

    if best_n <= 2500 and time.time() < binary_end and time.time() < deadline - 60:
        try:
            hb, cb = binary_swap_search(best_h, best_n, min(binary_end, deadline - 50), seed=int(seed) + 1)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h = hb.copy()
                best_c = cb
                best_n = best_n
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < binary_end and len(selected) > 0 and selected[0][1] <= 2500 and time.time() < deadline - 60:
        try:
            hb, cb = binary_swap_search(selected[0][0], selected[0][1], min(binary_end, deadline - 45), seed=int(seed) + 2)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h = hb.copy()
                best_c = cb
                best_n = selected[0][1]
        except Exception:
            pass

    final_end = deadline - 4.0
    if time.time() < final_end - 15:
        try:
            hp, cp = run_dense_lbfgs(best_h, best_n, final_end)
            if cp < best_c:
                best_h = hp.copy()
                best_c = cp
                best_n = best_n
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c = eval_c_exact(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(final_c), int(best_n)
```
